After creating a driver through the app's own **Create Driver** drawer, the tabs read `All (80) · Active (1) · Inactive (77) · On Leave (0) · Terminated (0)` — **the four tabs sum to 78 while All is 80**. **The Create Driver drawer defaults Status to `Probation`, and there is no `Probation` tab**, so every driver created through the UI lands by default in a bucket no status filter surfaces. A dispatcher or safety reviewer filtering by status never sees newly created drivers. **PROD CONFIRMS (`mdata.drivers`, USMCA, non-archived, `bypass_rls='lucia'`, `current_user=neondb_owner`, complete read `visible_all = n_live_tup = 181`):** Inactive **78** · **Probation 2** · Active 1. **FIX REQUIREMENT:** surface every status the create path can assign — either add a `Probation` tab or make the tab set derive from the status enum rather than a hardcoded four, so a future enum value cannot silently become invisible again. Deriving is preferred: a hardcoded list reproduces this defect on the next status added. Pairs with the existing **"Driver default drift"** card (backend default vs UI default, Probation) — same status, different symptom; fix them together, but note this one is about **visibility**, not the default value. **RESIDUAL, NOT GUESSED:** the tab math still does not fully tie — prod shows 78 non-archived `Inactive` vs tab 77, and non-archived total 81 vs `All (80)`, so a second narrower filter exists that the live-verifier lane did **not** isolate. **UNVERIFIED — needs live check** on that residual; the `Probation`-has-no-tab gap is fully proven and is the reportable defect. **GUARD:** assert the rendered status-tab set covers every value the driver status enum/create-path can produce; a snapshot test of four hardcoded tabs reproduces the miss. **Recorded by the live-verifier lane, which does not build.** Full record: `docs/audit/LIVE-TXN-BATTERY-2026-08-06.md` item 07. | — | **OPEN (moderate) — live-proven 2026-08-06, awaiting build** |
| **OPEN** `LV-AP-DUP` — **duplicate vendor bill accepted with no warning, and posted to GL twice** | — | C | **CC-1 / money** | **LIVE-PROVEN 2026-08-06 (CC-3 live-verifier lane) on deploy `e6343f4`, USMCA test entity.** Two `accounting.bills` rows now exist with the **same vendor, same `bill_number`, same amount, same `bill_date`** — `5a1d7268-a5d8-4bc4-9ffe-50187674dd19` and `55997ecb-2a81-4b01-ab70-c23f41d3829d`, created **10.3 s apart** (`16:41:28.897Z` / `16:41:39.161Z`), both `status='unpaid'`, both USMCA, both `qbo_bill_id IS NULL` (TMS-native). **BOTH POSTED to the GL, each balanced DR 5400 Truck Repairs & Maintenance / CR 2000 Accounts Payable $743.21** — so the ledger now carries **$1,486.42** of expense and A/P for a single $743.21 vendor invoice. The vendor was accepted with **no warning, no confirm, no override prompt**. **NOT a double-submit race** — the submissions are 10 s apart, so a disabled-button fix would NOT have prevented this; the gap is a missing duplicate-detection RULE. (A button-disable is still worth adding, but it is the lesser half.) **CONFIRMED ON PROD:** `accounting.bills` has **no UNIQUE index covering `bill_number`** (`pg_indexes` filtered to UNIQUE + `bill_number` → `NONE`). **STANDARD:** duplicate vendor-invoice detection is a baseline AP internal control — QuickBooks Online warns *"Bill number already exists for this vendor"*; McLeod and NetSuite block or require an explicit override. This is the classic duplicate-payment vector, and here it flows straight through to the ledger. **FIX REQUIREMENT:** on bill create/edit, detect an existing non-voided bill for the **same `operating_company_id` + vendor + `bill_number`** and require an explicit override to proceed (QBO-style warn-with-override; do NOT hard-block, carriers legitimately re-use numbers across vendors). Entity-scope the check — `bill_number` is per-entity, so the predicate must include `operating_company_id` or it will false-positive across entities. Record the override (who/when/reason) since it is an internal-control decision. Consider a partial UNIQUE index on `(operating_company_id, vendor_uuid, bill_number) WHERE voided_at IS NULL` ONLY if the override path is modelled first — a bare constraint would break legitimate overrides and cannot be added retroactively over existing dupes without a cleanup migration. **DO NOT delete or void the two test bills** — owner standing order 2026-08-06 keeps USMCA test records live until the system is certified end-to-end; they are the reproduction. **GUARD:** assert the create path performs the entity-scoped duplicate lookup, and that the override is persisted; a static grep for a constraint name would miss the application-layer rule entirely. Must be `--selftest`ed and mutation-proven. **Recorded by the live-verifier lane, which does not build.** Full record: `docs/audit/LIVE-TXN-BATTERY-2026-08-06.md` item 05. | — | **OPEN (major) — live-proven 2026-08-06, awaiting build** |
| **OPEN** `LV-CAT-500` — **21 of 22 generic catalog list endpoints return HTTP 500 on prod** | — | C/D | **CC-3 / mechanical+route** | **LIVE-PROVEN 2026-08-06 (CC-3 live-verifier lane) on deploy `e6343f4` (== tip of main), authenticated browser session vs `https://api.ih35dispatch.com`.** Surfaced running battery item 01 (create vendor on USMCA): the **Vendor type** picker renders **zero options** — only `+ Add new vendor type` — although USMCA has **8 active** `catalogs.vendor_types` rows. The catalog is not empty; **the endpoint throws.** **TWO DISTINCT ROOT CAUSES, both in `apps/backend/src/catalogs/generic-catalog.factory.ts`:** **(1) `t.deactivated_at` — 19 endpoints.** The list handler hard-codes `t.deactivated_at IS NULL` / `IS NOT NULL` into the WHERE clause (`generic-catalog.factory.ts:165-166`) whenever the `is_active` filter applies, but **19 of these catalog tables have no `deactivated_at` column** (verified on prod via `information_schema`). **(2) `t.name` — 2 endpoints.** `dbColumnForApiColumn` (`generic-catalog.factory.ts:70`) maps API `display_name` → physical **`"name"`** as its DEFAULT when a config omits `displayNameColumn`: `return config?.displayNameColumn ?? "name"`. `vendorTypesCatalogConfig` / `customerTypesCatalogConfig` omit it, but those tables physically have **`display_name`**, not `name`. The safe default is **identity (`display_name`)**, not `"name"` — note two configs legitimately set `displayNameColumn: "name"` (routes.ts:672,693), so flipping the default does not break them. **EVIDENCE — the partition is exact and mutually corroborating (19/2/1):** 19 tables missing `deactivated_at` ↔ exactly the 19 endpoints failing on `t.deactivated_at`; 2 tables missing `name` ↔ exactly the 2 failing on `t.name`; `equipment_types` (has BOTH `name` and `deactivated_at`) is the only one that works. **POSITIVE CONTROL PASSES:** `GET /api/v1/catalogs/fleet/equipment-types?operating_company_id=5c854333-…` → **200**, 5 rows, `{code:"DRY_VAN", operating_company_id:"5c854333-…"}` — so auth, entity scoping and the factory all work; these are column bugs, not a session/permission artifact. **FAILING (all 500, live):** `lumper_providers, accident_types, workplace_incident_types, leave_types, cash_advance_types, pm_intervals, repair_locations, work_order_templates, air_bag_catalog, battery_catalog, tire_catalog, trailer_parts, truck_parts, def_stations, fuel_stations, relay_accounts, toll_providers, load_trailer_equipment, mx_customs_brokers` (`t.deactivated_at`) + `vendor_types, customer_types` (`t.name`). **NOT a false-empty:** §0 completeness discriminator run on `catalogs.vendor_types` — `visible = n_live_tup = 24`, `n_tup_ins = 24`, `n_tup_del = 0`, `relrowsecurity/relforcerowsecurity = t`; per entity TRANSP 8 / TRK 8 / **USMCA 8** active, `opco IS NULL` = 0. **NOT expected state:** seeded reference catalogs, not import-origin rows — the 2026-08-04 origin ruling does not exempt them. **USER IMPACT:** every one of these pickers is silently empty across ALL THREE entities (the SQL fails before entity scoping is even reached), and the FE swallows the 500 and renders only `+ Add new`, so it reads as "no data seeded" rather than an error — this is why it survived. Blocks maintenance parts/tire/battery selection, fuel stations, toll providers, brokers, accident + incident types, leave and cash-advance types, PM intervals and WO templates. **FIX REQUIREMENT:** (a) make the `deactivated_at` predicate conditional on the column actually existing (or drive it off a per-config `softDeleteTimestampColumn`, defaulting to none) — do NOT add `deactivated_at` to 19 tables to satisfy the query; (b) change the `dbColumnForApiColumn` default from `?? "name"` to `?? "display_name"` (identity) and keep explicit `displayNameColumn: "name"` on the two configs that need it. **STANDARD:** §4 schema-reality — the service must be written against the real prod schema, never assumed column names; §0 verify-on-prod. **GUARD:** add a guard that, for EVERY `GenericCatalogConfig`, asserts each physical column the factory can emit (display-name alias, code alias, soft-delete column, `deactivated_at`) actually exists on `catalogs.<tableName>` — a static grep over route files reproduces this exact miss, so the guard must resolve config → physical columns. Must be `--selftest`ed and mutation-proven. **Recorded by the live-verifier lane, which does not build — CC-3 builder lane owns the fix.** Full record: `docs/audit/LIVE-TXN-BATTERY-2026-08-06.md` item 02. | — | **OPEN (major) — live-proven 2026-08-06, awaiting build** |
| **★★ OPEN — CRITICAL** `LV-BULK-DELIVER-NOLATCH` — **bulk "Mark delivered" stamps delivery evidence, tells the workflow spine the load delivered, and NEVER fires the revenue latch — and the CI guard is GREEN while it happens** | — | C | **CC-1 / money (fix) + CC-2 / guard** | **LIVE-PROVEN 2026-08-07 (CC-3 live-verifier lane) by code read + executed guard.** `dispatch/loads-bulk.routes.ts` action `set_status`: (a) **accepts the delivery statuses** — `setStatusPayloadSchema.transition` is `dispatchStatusSchema` (`load-state-machine.ts:3-14`) which includes `delivered_pending_docs` + `completed_docs_received`, and `PER_LOAD_ONLY_TRANSITIONS` (`:23`) fences off ONLY `abandoned`/`driver_walkoff`/`driver_no_show`; (b) **writes the status** `UPDATE mdata.loads SET status = $3::mdata.load_status_enum` (`:90-97`) from a runtime value, never a literal; (c) **STAMPS THE DELIVERY DEPARTURE** — `loadStatusRequiresDeliveryDepartureStamp` (`:99`) is exactly `DELIVERY_EVIDENCE_STATUSES.has(status)` (`stamp-final-delivery-departure.ts:27-29`) -> calls `stampFinalActiveDeliveryDeparture`; (d) emits the spine event (`:104`); (e) **NEVER references `latchOnDeliveryEvidence` or `postLoadRevenueLatch`.** That is verbatim the defect CLS-DISP-WIRE-07 exists to eliminate; the bulk path got the STAMP half of WIRE-07 and not the LATCH half. **THE FILE'S OWN COMMENT (`:19-23`) HALF-SAW IT:** *"Bulk set_status does NOT run those financial hooks … Route them to the per-load action instead of losing the side-effects"* — the author fenced off three statuses and **the delivery statuses fall through the very hole that comment was written to close.** **★ THE GUARD IS GREEN WHILE THE HOLE IS OPEN — EXECUTED, NOT ARGUED.** `scripts/verify-delivery-evidence-latch-wired.mjs` scopes files via `assignsEvidenceStatus()`, a regex demanding a STRING-LITERAL assignment `(?:=|\?|:)\s*["']delivered_pending_docs["']`. The bulk route binds a VARIABLE, so nothing matches; its one literal sits inside `new Set([… , "delivered_pending_docs", …])` (`:42`) preceded by a **comma**, which the regex rejects. **The file is never even considered.** Live run 2026-08-07: `file mentions delivered_pending_docs: true` / `guard matcher says it ASSIGNS status: null` / `file references the latch: false` / `guard IN SCOPE: false`; and `node scripts/verify-delivery-evidence-latch-wired.mjs` prints **"OK — every delivery-evidence transition fires the revenue latch", exit 0 — a statement that is FALSE.** The guard's own docstring promises to catch *"a bulk driver tool"* as one of three named examples. **It does not.** This is "law = enforced guard or it is not law" in its most dangerous form: **not a missing guard, but a GREEN, TRUSTED guard while the law it encodes is being broken.** **★ FIX — DO NOT MERGE WITH `LV-REVREC-NOT-FIRING`.** Different path, different cause: item 40 = latch present but fires at the wrong instant; item 41 = latch absent. **Fixing 40 alone leaves the bulk path dark; fixing 41 alone REPRODUCES 40's timing bug in a second place**, because the bulk route stamps on its own `client` in the same transaction — a naively added latch there hits the identical invisibility. **Item 41's fix MUST adopt item 40's after-commit ordering.** **GUARD:** widen the matcher to non-literal writes — key on **`stampFinalActiveDeliveryDeparture`**, which is the definitional signal that a path delivers a load, is a single shared helper, and cannot be written as a variable; pair it with item 40's live ledger assertion (a static guard alone cannot catch 40; a ledger guard alone would not name the file). **Recorded by the live-verifier lane, which does not build.** Full record: `docs/audit/LIVE-TXN-BATTERY-2026-08-06.md` item 41. | — | **OPEN (CRITICAL) — live-proven 2026-08-07** |
| **OPEN** `LV-STOP-ZIP-DROPPED` — **the book-load wizard silently drops the stop ZIP code; 0 of 18 stops in the entire table have one** | — | C | **CC-2 / mechanical** | **LIVE-PROVEN 2026-08-07 (CC-3 live-verifier lane), USMCA, booked through the real UI.** Booked `L-20260806-0008` (`1d5d8733-5891-44fc-891f-078ced5ae66f`), $1,875.50. **I typed `78040` (pickup) and `78205` (delivery) and SAW both rendered in the form fields.** On prod immediately after: `address_line1` persisted on BOTH stops (`1200 Santa Ursula Ave`, `500 E Cesar E Chavez Blvd`), `city` persisted on BOTH (`Laredo`, `San Antonio`), **`postal_code` NULL on both.** **COMPLETENESS DISCRIMINATOR APPLIED:** `mdata.load_stops` non-empty `postal_code` = **0**, total = **18**, `n_live_tup` = **18** (visible == live -> a REAL zero, not an RLS mask). By contrast `city` is non-empty on **10/18** and `state` on **9/18** — **those persist when supplied; `postal_code` never has, for any row that has ever existed.** `information_schema` confirms `postal_code` is the ONLY candidate column (address cols = `address_line1`, `city`, `state`, `postal_code`; there is no separate `zip`), so this is not a wrong-column read. **WHY THIS IS A MONEY DEFECT, NOT COSMETIC:** the section is titled **"STOPS · PC\*MILER ROUTING"** and the booking screen showed **PRACTICAL 0 · SHORTEST 0 · DEADHEAD 0 · RPM —**. Postal code is the PC\*MILER routing key. Without it, **driver pay per mile** (the screen itself states *"Shortest miles (yellow) used for driver pay"*), **fuel planning + ETA**, and **IFTA jurisdiction miles** are structurally unreachable — and every diesel expense must FK to a load (G18). A load that can never compute miles cannot settle a per-mile driver correctly or produce a defensible IFTA return. **GUARD:** assert a stop created through the booking path with a supplied postal code persists it, plus a live assertion that TMS-native stops are not 100% NULL on `postal_code` (scoped TMS-native per the origin test — imported stops may legitimately lack one). **Recorded by the live-verifier lane, which does not build.** Full record: `docs/audit/LIVE-TXN-BATTERY-2026-08-06.md` item 43. | — | **OPEN (major) — live-proven 2026-08-07** |
| **OPEN** `LV-DISPATCH-TOAST-LIES` — **UI says "Load booked and dispatched" after an audited DOT override; the load was never dispatched** | — | C | **CC-2 / mechanical** | **LIVE-PROVEN 2026-08-07 (CC-3 live-verifier lane), USMCA.** After `Override & dispatch` on `L-20260806-0008`, the UI showed a green **"Load booked and dispatched"** toast. On prod that load is **`assigned_not_dispatched`** — `created_at 02:05:48`, `updated_at 02:05:51`, unchanged on re-query minutes later. **NOT a naming artifact:** `mdata.load_status_enum` contains a real, distinct **`dispatched`** member (verified via `enum_range`: … `assigned`, **`dispatched`**, `at_pickup`, `in_transit` …, alongside `assigned_not_dispatched`). The load sits in the PRE-dispatch state while the UI reports the POST-dispatch one. **WHY IT MATTERS BEYOND COSMETICS:** the override was recorded specifically to permit **dispatch** past two DOT blockers (`WF-CDL-MISSING` no CDL expiry on file, `WF-MED-CARD-MISSING` no DOT medical card). A dispatcher reading that toast believes a truck is rolling under an audited override; the record says it never left. **An override audit trail attesting to an action that did not happen is worse than no override at all** — that is precisely what a DOT/FMCSA reviewer or an insurer reads. **GUARD:** the success toast must report the status the SERVER actually returned, never an assumed one. **SIDE EFFECT ON THIS LANE:** this blocks the end-to-end reproduction of `LV-REVREC-NOT-FIRING` — the load cannot leave `assigned_not_dispatched`, so the lifecycle stalls at step 2. Item 40s root cause stands on code + prod evidence; the live end-to-end replay is **NOT yet done and is stated as such.** **Recorded by the live-verifier lane, which does not build.** Full record: `docs/audit/LIVE-TXN-BATTERY-2026-08-06.md` item 44. | — | **OPEN (major) — live-proven 2026-08-07** |
| **FIXED by CC-3 (guard) — NOT MERGED** `LV-LATCH-GUARD-BLIND` — **the delivery-latch guard was GREEN while the law it encodes was broken; matcher widened, now catches it** | — | C | **CC-3 / CI-guard (done) -> CC-1 must wire the bulk latch before it can go green** | **FIXED 2026-08-07 in the same session it was found (CLAUDE.md §3: mechanical / CI-guards is CC-3s own lane, non-financial).** `scripts/verify-delivery-evidence-latch-wired.mjs` `assignsEvidenceStatus()` widened from literal-only to THREE signals: (1) the original literal assignment; (2) **`stampFinalActiveDeliveryDeparture(` is called** — the DEFINITIONAL signal that a path delivers a load, a single shared helper, and **unlike a status string a function call cannot be hidden behind a variable**, so it survives the validate->map->bind pattern that defeated the literal scan; (3) belt-and-braces `UPDATE mdata.loads` + `SET status` + the file names an evidence status. Signal 3 requires `SET status` SPECIFICALLY so `mdata/driver-team.service.ts` (writes `SET team_id`, only READS the status in an `ACTIVE_LOAD_STATUSES` filter list) is **not** false-positived — verified by reading that file before shipping the rule. **Selftest 6 -> 8 cases:** case6 = the exact parameterized shape that was invisible **must be caught**; case7 = the same shape WITH a latch **must stay clean** (proves the widening is not an unconditional flag); case8 = a writer of a DIFFERENT column that merely reads an evidence status **must not** be flagged. **Removed the old case6 that asserted the real tree was clean** — it conflated "is the matcher correct" with "is the repo compliant", so a genuine defect would surface as a confusing selftest failure instead of a plain guard failure. **EXECUTED 2026-08-07: `--selftest` PASS exit 0; real run exit 1 flagging EXACTLY ONE file — `dispatch/loads-bulk.routes.ts` — the real defect, with no false positives.** **★ THE GUARD IS NOW RED AND THAT IS THE POINT — BUT IT IS NOT MERGED.** It lives on `audit/cc3-live-battery-20260806`. Merging it to `main` turns a required check red for **EVERY** lane until CC-1 wires the bulk latch. **This lane did not impose that on other lanes unilaterally.** Correct sequence: **CC-1 wires the bulk latch USING ITEM 40s AFTER-COMMIT ORDERING -> the guard goes green -> both land together.** That is sequencing, not deferring — the guard is the forcing function and it is already written. Merging it red immediately is available and is the owners call. | **★ OPEN** `LV-LOAD-DETAIL-SHOWS-UNASSIGNED` — **the load detail drawer shows a FULLY ASSIGNED load as "Driver: Unassigned" / unit "—" / trip type "—"; root-caused to an 18-column view** | — | C | **CC-2 / mechanical** | **LIVE-PROVEN 2026-08-07 (CC-3 live-verifier lane), USMCA, `L-20260806-0008` (`1d5d8733-5891-44fc-891f-078ced5ae66f`).** Drawer renders `TRIP TYPE —` / `TRUCK UNIT —` / **`DRIVER Unassigned`** while the **load board on the same screen** shows `USMCA-001` for that load. **MY FIRST HYPOTHESIS (wizard failed to persist) WAS WRONG AND THE DB CHECK CAUGHT IT** — prod has `assigned_primary_driver_id=88c04cf5…` (**Juan USMCA-Battery**), `assigned_unit_id=bb1e77ab…` (**USMCA-001**), `trip_type=`**`NB`**. **The wizard saved correctly; this is a READ/DISPLAY defect, not a write defect** — a builder told "the wizard loses the driver" would rewrite a form that works. **ROOT CAUSE, PROVEN:** `GET /api/v1/dispatch/loads/:id` (`dispatch/loads.routes.ts:663`, observed live **200**) does `SELECT l.*` **FROM `views.dispatch_load_with_driver_status`** — NOT from `mdata.loads`. That view has **18 columns total**; per prod `information_schema.columns`: `trip_type` **ABSENT**, `assigned_unit_number` **ABSENT**, `assigned_primary_driver_name` **ABSENT**, while `assigned_primary_driver_id` and `assigned_unit_id` ARE present. **Exactly the three fields that render wrong are exactly the three the view does not expose** — `undefined` -> `—`, and `assigned_primary_driver_name ?? "Unassigned"` -> **"Unassigned"** (`LoadDetailDrawer.tsx:370/372/374`). **SMOKING GUN — AN ASYMMETRY IN ONE QUERY:** the same handler DOES resolve a driver name, but only the **SECONDARY** one (`LEFT JOIN mdata.drivers sd ON sd.id = l.assigned_secondary_driver_id` -> `assigned_secondary_driver_name`). **There is NO equivalent join for the PRIMARY driver and none for the unit** — so the team-driver field works while the primary-driver field, the one that matters on every load, does not. **COROBORATION the defect is local to this endpoint:** the load board renders `USMCA-001` correctly for the same load. **INTERNALLY INCONSISTENT IN ONE FILE:** `LoadDetailDrawer.tsx:712` and `:849` use `load.assigned_primary_driver_id`, which IS in the payload and DOES work — the drawer itself proves the ID is available; only the display block reached for names never sent. **WHY NOT COSMETIC:** a dispatcher sees "Driver: Unassigned" on a load with a driver and truck committed -> double-booking, re-assigning a covered load, believing a unit is free; and it violates total-connectivity (§10a) — a record that IS linked is displayed as unlinked, breaking forward drill-through on that records primary screen. **FIX (preferred, lowest risk, matches code already present):** in the detail handler add `trip_type` and LEFT JOIN `mdata.drivers`/`mdata.units` to compute `assigned_primary_driver_name` + `assigned_unit_number` — **mirror for the primary driver exactly what the query already does for the secondary**, entity-scoped with the same `operating_company_id` predicate. Alternative (higher blast radius): widen the shared view — remember `CREATE OR REPLACE VIEW` is append-only per §2. **GUARD:** a CONTRACT test on the endpoint response — assert a non-null driver name and unit number whenever `assigned_primary_driver_id`/`assigned_unit_id` are set. **A guard that only checked the columns exist on `mdata.loads` would PASS here** (they do exist; the endpoint never selects them) — same shape as `LV-BULK-DELIVER-NOLATCH`: the naive guard confirms the wrong thing. **RELATED, NOT CONFLATED:** `LoadDetailDrawer.tsx:379` user-facing copy and the handler comment both reference **`loads.trailer_id`**, a column that **does not exist** (only `trailer_type` does) — the handler comment itself records that a prior join on that non-existent column 500d every load-detail fetch (42703) and broke the cancel flow; the stale reference survives in UI copy. **NOT CLAIMED:** whether this shares a cause with `LV-DISPATCH-TOAST-LIES` — that action ran from the booking wizard, not this drawer. **UNVERIFIED — needs its own check**; recorded separately on purpose. **Recorded by the live-verifier lane, which does not build.** Full record: `docs/audit/LIVE-TXN-BATTERY-2026-08-06.md` items 45 + 46. **★ CORROBORATED ON FOUR SURFACES (item 46):** the SAME record renders driver **Juan USMCA-Battery** + unit **USMCA-001** correctly in the **List** view, the **Kanban** card, AND the **Assignment** view — and wrong ONLY in the load detail drawer. **Three right, one wrong. That kills the data-problem explanation outright** (a data problem would break all four) and pins the defect to `GET /api/v1/dispatch/loads/:id`. One screenshot captures the contradiction in a SINGLE frame: the drawer reads "Unassigned" while the board row behind it reads `USMCA-001`. | — | **OPEN (major) — live-proven 2026-08-07, corroborated 4 surfaces** |
| **★ OPEN** `LV-CANCEL-VOIDS-STATUS-ONLY` — **cancelling a load voids its invoice by STATUS ONLY and never stamps `voided_at`; $2,450.00 of voided A/R is carried as LIVE** | — | C | **CC-1 / money** | **LIVE-PROVEN 2026-08-07 (CC-3 live-verifier lane) on Neon prod, USMCA — found while taking an AR baseline, before creating anything.** `INV-2026-00005`: **`status=`void`, `voided_at` NULL, `void_reason` NULL**, `total_cents` **245000**, `amount_open_cents` **245000**; its `source_load_id` = `a0b1df25-…` = **`L-20260806-0005`, the CANCELLED load**. Whole-entity count: exactly **1** row with `status=`void` AND `voided_at IS NULL`, overstating **$2,450.00** on BOTH total and open. **HOW IT DISTORTS A LIVE READ:** under the §2 WORM convention "live" = `voided_at IS NULL`, so `live_total_cents` = **552550** = 187550 + **245000 (VOID)** + 120000 — **a voided invoice carried as live A/R**, and `amount_open_cents` still 245000 so AR aging shows it OPEN. **ROOT CAUSE — THE TWO VOID PATHS DISAGREE.** Canonical route `accounting/invoices.routes.ts:846-856` sets **status + `voided_at = now()` + `void_reason`**. Load-cancellation path `dispatch/cancellation.service.ts:181-187` sets **`status = `void`, updated_at, updated_by_user_id` — NO `voided_at`, NO `void_reason`.** **CORROBORATED BY THE DATA: every invoice voided through the proper route (`INV-…001`, `…002`, `…004`) carries BOTH a `voided_at` and a descriptive `void_reason`; the one voided by load cancellation carries NEITHER. The split is perfectly clean.** **★ SCOPE — THIS IS NOT A GL DEFECT, AND THAT CHANGES THE FIX.** `gl_postings` for `INV-2026-00005` = **0** — a `proforma` was never posted, so there is **NO GL residue and NO reversing entry is owed**; the trial balance is unaffected. **The correct fix is to STAMP `voided_at`, NOT to post a reversal.** Treating this like the GL-residue class (`LV-VOID-NO-REVERSAL`) would post an entry against a document that never hit the ledger — **inventing financial data.** Damage is confined to the A/R subledger and every report built on `voided_at IS NULL`. **RELATIONSHIP TO `LV-AP-OPEN-INCLUDES-VOIDED` — SAME SYMPTOM, DIFFERENT MECHANISM:** that one was the open-AP QUERY not excluding voided rows over bills that DID carry `voided_at`; **here the ROW ITSELF is mis-stamped, so even a correctly-written query counts it.** Different causes, different fixes — kept as separate cards on purpose; merging them sends a fixer to the wrong layer. **FIX:** in `cancellation.service.ts` set `voided_at = now()` + a `void_reason` (the cancellation reason) in the SAME UPDATE, matching the canonical route. **KEEP the `status = `proforma`` predicate** — the surrounding comments correctly explain a NON-proforma invoice must not be auto-voided (a TONU/cancellation charge may be genuinely owed) and a driver bill is deliberately never auto-voided. **That reasoning is sound and must be preserved; the bug is ONLY the missing stamp.** **ALSO BACKFILL the existing row** or the guard stays red — safe (test entity, no GL impact), but this lane does not build. **GUARD:** assert `accounting.invoices` has ZERO rows with `status=`void` AND `voided_at IS NULL` (and the same invariant wherever the status+timestamp void pattern is used). **Live data assertion — a static scan cannot catch it.** **ALSO OBSERVED (PASS):** `INV-2026-00006` auto-created for `L-20260806-0008` at $1,875.50, status `proforma` — the booking wizard proforma pipeline WORKS. **Recorded by the live-verifier lane, which does not build.** Full record: `docs/audit/LIVE-TXN-BATTERY-2026-08-06.md` item 47. | — | **OPEN (major) — live-proven 2026-08-07** |
| **★ OPEN** `LV-AR-OPEN-INCLUDES-VOIDED` — **the A/R invoice list counts VOID invoices in both "Total billed" and "Open"; Open overstated $2,452.00** | — | C | **CC-1 / money** | **LIVE-PROVEN 2026-08-07 (CC-3 live-verifier lane) in the running app at `/accounting/invoices`, USMCA — visible on screen and confirmed by arithmetic against that same screens rows.** Header reads **"Total billed: $5,527.50 · Open: $5,277.50 · Rows: 6 of 6"** (no filter). Rows: INV-…006 proforma $1,875.50/$1,875.50; **INV-…005 VOID $2,450.00/$2,450.00**; **INV-…004 VOID $0.00/$0.00**; INV-…003 partial $1,200.00/$950.00; **INV-…002 VOID $1.00/$1.00**; **INV-…001 VOID $1.00/$1.00**. `1875.50+2450+0+1200+1+1 = `**5527.50** = "Total billed" EXACTLY; `1875.50+2450+0+950+1+1 = `**5277.50** = "Open" EXACTLY. **The four VOID invoices contribute $2,452.00 to reported Open.** True non-void open = `1875.50+950 = `**$2,825.50**. **TWO DISTINCT FAULTS ON ONE SCREEN:** (1) per row, a void invoice still shows a NON-ZERO Open — a voided document has no open balance by definition; (2) those values are then SUMMED into "Open", an A/R balance concept. **★ THIS IS NOT `LV-CANCEL-VOIDS-STATUS-ONLY`, AND THE DIFFERENCE DECIDES THE FIX.** That one is a DATA defect (one row missing `voided_at`). **This is a QUERY defect: `INV-…001`/`…002`/`…004` DO carry a proper `voided_at` (prod-verified `2026-08-05 23:16:48.664…`, `23:16:48.299…`, `23:14:59.653…`) and are STILL counted.** **Fixing the data card alone would NOT move this number** — the aggregation is evidently not filtering on `voided_at` at all. Both fixes are required and they live in different places. **FAIR READING OF THE DESIGN:** showing void rows in the REGISTER is legitimate (WORM — void, never delete; the row must stay visible). **That is not the complaint.** The complaint is that a BALANCE labelled "Open" includes them. Even in a register that deliberately displays voided documents, a voided invoices open amount is **$0.00** and the Open total must exclude it. **FLAGGED, NOT CLAIMED AS A DEFECT:** `INV-…006` is a **proforma** contributing $1,875.50 to both figures. A proforma is not yet an issued receivable, so whether it belongs in Total billed/Open is a **POLICY question** — the two-event model treats billing as the POD event. Named so whoever fixes the void filter **decides the proforma question explicitly rather than by accident.** **RELATIONSHIP TO `LV-AP-OPEN-INCLUDES-VOIDED`: this is its exact A/R TWIN with the SAME mechanism** (an open-balance aggregate that does not exclude voided documents) — unlike the cancel-stamp card, which shares only the symptom. **Two sides of the ledger, one missing predicate.** Fix both in the same pass and sweep the pattern elsewhere (aging reports, dashboards, cash-flow projections). **FIX:** exclude voided invoices from both aggregates and zero the per-row Open for a voided document. **GUARD:** assert the A/R open aggregate equals the sum over NON-VOID invoices only — a live assertion computed two independent ways, which is exactly how this was caught. **Recorded by the live-verifier lane, which does not build.** Full record: `docs/audit/LIVE-TXN-BATTERY-2026-08-06.md` item 48. | — | **OPEN (major) — live-proven 2026-08-07** |
| **OPEN** `LV-BILL-NO-DISPLAY-ID` — **TMS-native BILLS never get a `display_id` (13 of 13, all entities); invoices and payments do** | — | C | **CC-1 / money** | **LIVE-PROVEN 2026-08-07 (CC-3 live-verifier lane) on Neon prod. ORIGIN TEST APPLIED — the raw count would be WRONG to report.** `display_id` is NULL on all **16,258** `accounting.bills` rows, but per the origin ruling imported rows are legitimately different, so that number is meaningless. **Classified by origin:** TRK 13,051 bills = 13,050 QBO-cloned + **1 TMS-native**; TRANSP 3,196 = 3,195 cloned + **1 TMS-native**; USMCA 11 = **0 cloned, 11 TMS-native** (`source_system=`tms``, zero `qbo_bill_id`). **TMS-native WITH a display_id: 0 in EVERY entity.** => **Every TMS-native bill that has ever existed — 13 of 13 — has a NULL `display_id`; the 16,245 QBO clones are EXCLUDED from the claim, their NULL is expected state.** **THE CONTRAST IS THE PROOF IT IS A GAP, NOT A DESIGN CHOICE:** in the SAME entity, TMS-native **invoices carry a display_id 6 of 6** (`INV-2026-00001`…`00006`) and **payments 2 of 2** (`PMT-2026-00001/2`). **Bills are the only money document in the system without a human-readable identifier.** **WHY IT MATTERS:** a bill is what you argue about with a vendor, attach to an approval, cite in a dispute and hand an auditor; it can currently only be cited by raw UUID — which is exactly what the app URL falls back to (`/accounting/bills/7ccd431e-8e85-433a-a5e0-4425011ffb5e`). **NOT A WORM CONFLICT (stated because it looks like one):** "void ALWAYS by UUID, never display_id" governs WHICH KEY AN OPERATION USES, not whether a human ID exists — invoices are voided by UUID AND carry `INV-…` IDs. **FIX:** server-generated `display_id` for TMS-native bills, matching the existing convention. **GUARD:** assert every TMS-native (`qbo_bill_id IS NULL`) bill has a non-null `display_id`, **scoped to TMS-native rows by the origin test** — a guard over the whole table would be RED FOREVER on expected state (the `expected-state-recorded-as-failure` anti-pattern). **Recorded by the live-verifier lane, which does not build.** Full record: `docs/audit/LIVE-TXN-BATTERY-2026-08-06.md` item 50. | — | **OPEN (minor-major) — live-proven 2026-08-07** |
| **★ OPEN** `LV-VOID-INVARIANT-BOTH-WAYS` — **the `status=void` <-> `voided_at` pairing is broken in BOTH directions; my own item-47 guard was HALF A GUARD** | — | C | **CC-1 / money** | **LIVE-PROVEN 2026-08-07 (CC-3 live-verifier lane), USMCA.** Query for `(voided_at IS NOT NULL AND status <> `void`) OR (status = `void` AND voided_at IS NULL)` on `accounting.bills` -> **4 rows**, all the out-of-band-voided bills (identical `voided_at 2026-08-07 00:33:50.999787+00`), **every one still `status = `unpaid``.** **★ EXACT MIRROR OF `LV-CANCEL-VOIDS-STATUS-ONLY`:** there an invoice has **status `void` / `voided_at` NULL**; here bills have **`voided_at` SET / status `unpaid`**. **The SAME invariant is violated in OPPOSITE directions by two different mechanisms** — either half alone reads as a one-off; together they prove **the pairing is not enforced at all**, so any writer can set one field and not the other. **★ SELF-CORRECTION — I AM FIXING MY OWN GUARD SPEC:** item 47 proposed *assert zero rows with `status=`void` AND `voided_at IS NULL`*. **That is only HALF a guard — it passes cleanly on all four bills here, which are broken the other way.** **The correct guard is BIDIRECTIONAL:** flag `(voided_at IS NOT NULL AND status <> `void`) OR (status = `void` AND voided_at IS NULL)`, per table using the status+timestamp void pattern. Applied to `accounting.bills` + `accounting.invoices` today it reddens on **5 rows** (4 bills + 1 invoice) and **each needs a DIFFERENT correction**. **BETTER STILL — ENFORCE IN THE DATABASE** (CHECK constraint or trigger) so no future writer can set one without the other: a guard catches it after the fact, a constraint makes it impossible. **THIS EXPLAINS A SYMPTOM ALREADY ON THE BOARD:** those four voided bills reading `status=`unpaid`` is precisely why voided money keeps appearing in open-A/P figures — the status field, which the A/P views key off, still says the bill is owed. **`LV-AP-OPEN-INCLUDES-VOIDED` and this card are the SAME ROOT CAUSE SEEN FROM TWO ENDS.** **SUPERSEDES the guard wording in `LV-CANCEL-VOIDS-STATUS-ONLY` — build the bidirectional form, not the one-sided one.** **Recorded by the live-verifier lane, which does not build.** Full record: `docs/audit/LIVE-TXN-BATTERY-2026-08-06.md` items 51 + 63. **★ MEASURED AND BOUNDED 2026-08-07 (item 63) — THIS MAKES THE PERMANENT FIX CHEAP.** Swept EVERY populated money table for the invariant in BOTH directions: **10 tables, 75,459 rows measured.** **Violations exist in exactly TWO tables and total FIVE ROWS:** `accounting.bills` **4** (stamped, status not void) and `accounting.invoices` **1** (status void, not stamped). **The other eight are 100% CLEAN** — `expenses` (27,072), `bank_transactions` (11,072), `bill_payments` (6,546), `journal_entries` (1,802), `recon_runs`, `recon_exceptions`, `driver_settlements`, `work_orders`. **=> The pairing is maintained correctly almost everywhere; these are two specific broken WRITE-PATHS (bills voided out-of-band by direct SQL; the load-cancel path that sets status without stamping), NOT a missing convention.** **★ WHY THIS UNLOCKS THE PERMANENT FIX:** a DB-level CHECK/trigger enforcing `status=`void` <-> void-timestamp IS NOT NULL` — which no future writer can forget — needs **5 rows corrected, not a 75k backfill.** That removes the usual reason this class gets deferred. Per owner law (never patch): correct the 5 rows **and** add the constraint **and** fix the two write-paths in ONE change — a guard catches the next occurrence after the fact, **a constraint makes it impossible.** **It also tells CC-1 where NOT to look:** no sweep of expenses, bank transactions, bill payments or journal entries is warranted. **MUST BE PER-TABLE, NOT COPY-PASTE:** the column is `revoked_at` on `bill_payments`, `voided_at` on invoices/bills/expenses, `voided_reason` vs `void_reason` for the reason field, and **`accounting.bills` carries BOTH `voided_at` AND `revoked_at`** (two void timestamps on one table — its own latent trap). A templated constraint applied blindly WILL target the wrong column on at least one table. | — | **OPEN (major) — bounded to 5 rows 2026-08-07** |

| **★★ OPEN — HIGH** `LV-BILLPAY-VOID-NO-REVERSAL` — **voiding a bill payment does NOT post the reversing entry the UI EXPLICITLY PROMISES; GL keeps $33.40 of cash that never left the bank** | — | C | **CC-1 / money — HIGH** | **LIVE-PROVEN 2026-08-07 (CC-3 live-verifier lane) on a payment I created MYSELF minutes earlier, with a PRE-VOID BASELINE CAPTURED — a controlled before/after, not an inference.** **EXPERIMENT DESIGNED TO ISOLATE VOID FROM POSTING:** item 53 proved this same A/P path POSTS correctly, so a void failure here cannot be blamed on a broken poster. Per the standing order (multiples, reverse exactly ONE, siblings live) I first created a sibling: `8b68a9d7` **$33.40** (JE `4da3efbd`) and `70299eff` **$12.60** (JE `6fe7c21a`). **Pre-void baseline:** bill `paid_cents` **4600**; `bill_payment` postings **4** totalling **9200¢**. Then voided ONLY `8b68a9d7` via the app panel, which states verbatim: *"Voiding posts an equal-and-opposite **reversing entry** and keeps the audit trail. This cant be undone."* **RESULT — 3 of 4 invariants PASS, the ACCOUNTING one FAILS:** WORM ✅ (row preserved, `status=`void``, `revoked_at 2026-08-07 02:48:58`, `revoked_reason` stored verbatim); sibling untouched ✅ (`70299eff` still `posted`, 2 postings); subledger rolled back ✅ (`paid_cents` **4600 -> 1260**, UI open $42.77 -> **$76.17**); **REVERSING ENTRY ❌ — `bill_payment` postings STILL 4, STILL 9200¢, byte-identical to the pre-void baseline. NOTHING was created at the void timestamp.** Full posting list is only the original `8b68a9d7` DR `2000`/CR `1295` $33.40 at **02:41:42** and the siblings $12.60 at **02:47:47**. **There is no 02:48 entry.** **★ ACCOUNTING CONSEQUENCE:** GL DR `2000` A/P = `3340+1260` = **$46.00** and CR `1295` Relay Fuel Wallet = **$46.00**, while subledger `paid_cents` = **$12.60**. **A/P control (GL) understated by $33.40 vs the subledger, and THE BANK IS CREDITED $33.40 FOR CASH THAT NEVER LEFT** — the payment was voided. **The bank side is the worse half: a bank reconciliation will not tie, and the discrepancy is INVISIBLE from the A/P screens because the subledger looks correct.** **WHY THIS IS UNUSUALLY STRONG:** (1) **the UI makes an explicit accounting promise and does not keep it** — not a silent gap; a user reading that panel has every reason to believe the ledger was corrected; (2) **the other half of the same sentence IS honoured** (audit trail kept), so the path was written with care and the reversal is a SPECIFIC omission, not general neglect; (3) it **isolates cleanly** — same path posts correctly on create (item 53), fails to reverse on void, so there is a known-good reference right beside it; (4) controlled before/after on a row I created, so no origin ambiguity and no RLS doubt. **RELATIONSHIP TO `LV-VOID-NO-REVERSAL` — RELATED BUT MUCH STRONGER EVIDENCE:** that card covers $1,643.21 of residue from bills voided OUT-OF-BAND BY DIRECT SQL, where one could argue the service was simply bypassed. **This is the IN-APP void path, on a freshly created payment, with the reversal explicitly advertised — and it still does not reverse. It removes the "they bypassed the service" explanation entirely.** **FIX:** the bill-payment void path must post the equal-and-opposite entry (DR bank / CR A/P) **through the existing poster** — the create side already builds DR `2000` / CR bank, so the reversal is that entry INVERTED; link via `reverses_je_id`/`reversed_by_je_id` as the JE-void path does. **Write no new GL math.** **GUARD:** assert that for every `bill_payments` row with `revoked_at IS NOT NULL`, the NET GL effect of its `source_transaction_id` is ZERO. **★ SUB-FINDING — A THIRD VOID-COLUMN CONVENTION IN ONE SCHEMA, AND IT IS A LIVE TRAP:** `accounting.bill_payments` uses **`revoked_at`/`revoked_by_user_id`/`revoked_reason`**, while `accounting.invoices`, `accounting.bills` and `accounting.payments` use **`voided_at`**. **Every "exclude voided rows" fix on this board (`LV-AR-OPEN-INCLUDES-VOIDED`, `LV-AP-OPEN-INCLUDES-VOIDED`, `LV-PAYABLE-SELECTOR-OFFERS-VOIDED-BILLS`) is written by looking for `voided_at`. On `bill_payments` that column DOES NOT EXIST** — a developer applying the same pattern gets a column-does-not-exist error at best, or reaches for `status` and walks straight into the `LV-VOID-INVARIANT-BOTH-WAYS` trap. **Whoever fixes the void-filter family MUST be told this table is named differently.** **Recorded by the live-verifier lane, which does not build.** Full record: `docs/audit/LIVE-TXN-BATTERY-2026-08-06.md` items 54 + 58. **★★ SCOPE RESOLVED 2026-08-07 (item 58) — THIS IS NOT A SYSTEMIC VOID CLASS; IT IS SPECIFIC TO BILL PAYMENTS, AND THAT CHANGES THE FIX.** I created, posted and voided a FRESH invoice (`INV-2026-00007`, $313.90) as a controlled before/after. **Invoice void PASSES the GL half:** a reversing JE **`53415aef`** was created (memo *"Void reversal of invoice 135af1df…"*, at the void moment) with **REAL correct postings DR `4000` $313.90 / CR `1100` $313.90 — the exact inverse — and NET GL EFFECT ZERO on both accounts** (je_total 24 -> 25). **INDEPENDENT CONFIRMATION OF THIS CARD FROM A SECOND ANGLE:** a direct query for journal entries whose memo matches *"void reversal of bill payment"* returns **0** for this entity, even though a bill payment was voided an hour earlier — **the reversal JE simply does not exist**, while the invoice one plainly does using the same memo convention. **=> Void-without-reversal is NOT a class. Invoice void already contains a WORKING implementation of exactly what bill-payment void is missing. Had this gone unverified, CC-1 could have rewritten void handling across accounting; the correct fix is to COPY A NEIGHBOUR, not rebuild** (same lesson as item 53). **TWO LESSER DEFECTS DO REMAIN ON THE INVOICE PATH, both worth their own fix:** (1) **NO REVERSAL LINKAGE** — `reverses_je_id`/`reversed_by_je_id` are NULL on BOTH the original `e30baa6a` and the reversal `53415aef`, though `accounting.journal_entries` HAS both columns; **item 37s code-read claim is now LIVE-CONFIRMED**. The only thing connecting the two entries is a MEMO STRING containing the invoice UUID — **a memo is not a foreign key, and proving a reversal belongs to its original should never require text-parsing.** The JE-void path already writes both columns. (2) **REVERSAL POSTINGS ARE UNTAGGED, AND THIS SILENTLY CORRUPTS REPORTS** — the reversals postings carry `source_transaction_type = NULL` and `source_transaction_id = NULL`; **measured: `invoice_postings` stayed at 6 across the void.** **A revenue report grouping postings by `source_transaction_type = `invoice`` shows the full $313.90 as income and NEVER SEES THE REVERSAL.** The trial balance is fine (sums by account, nets zero) — **but any source-typed P&L, revenue-by-type or drill-through is overstated by the full amount of EVERY voided invoice.** This also explains the `(null)` source-type bucket already visible in the posting census: those rows are reversals sitting outside every source-typed report. **FIX:** populate both linkage columns mirroring JE-void, and carry `source_transaction_type`/`source_transaction_id` onto reversal postings. **GUARD:** assert every posting has a non-null `source_transaction_type`, and that a voided invoices postings net to zero WITHIN ITS SOURCE GROUPING, not merely by account. | — | **OPEN (HIGH) — scoped to bill payments 2026-08-07; invoice void proven correct** |

| **★ OPEN** `LV-BANK-TWO-SIGN-CONVENTIONS` — **`banking.bank_transactions.amount_cents` carries TWO mutually exclusive sign conventions (2,634 negative vs 108 positive credits), and the LIVE recon engine documents the wrong one** | — | C | **CC-1 / money (recon + banking)** | **LIVE-PROVEN 2026-08-07 (CC-3 live-verifier lane) on Neon prod, all three entities — READ-ONLY across entities; this lane never writes outside USMCA.** `is_credit=true` rows: **USMCA 69/69 negative; TRK 1,395/1,395 negative; TRANSP 1,278 = 1,170 negative + **108 POSITIVE**.** The 108 are not scattered — they are **ONE ACCOUNT**: every TRANSP checking account and the Platinum Card store credits 100% negative (1,170/1,170), while **Relay Fuel Wallet stores them 100% POSITIVE (108/108)**. **Whole-DB totals: 2,634 negative vs 108 positive.** **ORIGIN TEST APPLIED:** the 108 are a single coherent cohort — one account, contiguous window 2026-03-03 -> 2026-05-30, one description shape ("Relay deposit · card …NNNN. Card deposit. Manual De…"), totalling **$446,896.99** — a distinct FEED, not corruption. **=> Two mutually exclusive conventions coexist in one column and NOTHING IN THE SCHEMA RECORDS WHICH APPLIES TO WHICH ROW. Every future reader must already know Relay Fuel Wallet is special, or be wrong.** **★ EXPLICITLY NOT A DEFECT (checked before judging):** `banking/internal-wallet-balance.ts:97,198` does signed math `SUM(CASE WHEN is_credit THEN amount_cents ELSE -amount_cents END)` — which would be wrong under the negative convention — **but it is CORRECT**, because its own header scopes it to non-Plaid accounts and states *"the Relay Fuel Wallet is the only one today (verified prod 2026-07-16)"*. It runs ONLY over the account that stores credits positive. **NOT FILED.** **★ WHAT IS WRONG — a comment that contradicts prod on 96% of rows:** `accounting/recon/recon-engine.service.ts:188-193` says *"banking.bank_transactions stores amount_cents (bigint magnitude) + is_credit; normalize to a signed value"* and applies `CASE WHEN is_credit THEN amount_cents ELSE -amount_cents END`. **`amount_cents` is NOT a magnitude** — it is a magnitude on 108 rows and a SIGNED value on 2,634, so the premise is false for **96% of all credits in the database**, and `WHEN is_credit THEN amount_cents` yields a NEGATIVE value where the normalization intends positive. **AND THIS ENGINE IS LIVE, NOT PARKED: `TMS_QBO_RECON_ENABLED` = true for TRANSP, TRK AND USMCA** (prod `lib.feature_flag_overrides`). The accounting standard calls reconciliation *"the correctness spine … the daily correctness test"* — so a sign premise false for 96% of credits sits in the one component whose job is catching errors. **★ WHAT I AM NOT CLAIMING:** line 40 of that file says *"caller normalizes both sides the same way"* — **if the QBO side uses the identical mistaken expression the inversion CANCELS and matching still works.** Whether real exceptions are produced depends on the other side of the comparison, **which I did NOT trace — UNVERIFIED. CC-1 must confirm before treating this as a live mis-match rather than a false premise. I will not assert a dollar impact I have not measured.** **FIX:** make the convention EXPLICIT rather than assumed — normalize on write so `amount_cents` is a true magnitude everywhere (with `is_credit` the sole direction carrier), or record the per-account convention in the schema. **Note `posting-engine.service.ts:1677` and `bank-feed-gl-posting.service.ts:8` ALREADY DECLARE THE LAW — "DIRECTION IS DRIVEN ONLY BY is_credit — NEVER by the sign of amount_cents" — and the recon engine's normalization does exactly what that law forbids.** **GUARD:** assert ONE sign convention across the table, or that the Relay feed is the only registered exception. **Recorded by the live-verifier lane, which does not build.** Full record: `docs/audit/LIVE-TXN-BATTERY-2026-08-06.md` item 61. | — | **OPEN (major) — live-proven 2026-08-07** |
| **★ OPEN** `LV-BANK-BANNER-SAYS-FLAG-OFF` — **the banking page tells operators "Categorize tags are not ledger posts / BANK_FEED_GL_POSTING_ENABLED stays OFF" while the flag is ON for ALL THREE entities and categorize IS posting balanced JEs** | — | C | **CC-2 / mechanical (FE copy) — flag to CC-1 (GL-effect misstatement)** | **LIVE-PROVEN 2026-08-07 (CC-3 live-verifier lane), USMCA.** `/banking/transactions` renders a standing banner: *"Categorize tags are not ledger posts — `BANK_FEED_GL_POSTING_ENABLED` stays OFF by default … that alone does not post a balanced TMS JE … the flag-gated bank-feed poster is a separate path (default OFF)."* **PROD SAYS THE OPPOSITE:** `lib.feature_flags.default_enabled` = **false** (the only thing the banner describes), but `lib.feature_flag_overrides` = **TRUE for TRANSP, TRK AND USMCA**. **The flag is ON for all three entities**, and the default is the one value that does NOT govern, because the poster resolves the per-entity override first. **POSTING IS DEMONSTRABLY HAPPENING — BY THE BANNERS OWN EVIDENTIARY STANDARD.** The banner correctly warns that `matched_journal_entry_id` is NOT proof, **so I did not rely on it.** Independent measurement on USMCA: **4 categorized transactions, 4 with a JE link, and 8 `accounting.journal_entry_postings` with `source_transaction_type = `bank_categorization`` — two per transaction, i.e. BALANCED PAIRS, tagged with the bank-feed posters own source type.** That is not a recon match/link. **The banners stated disproof condition is satisfied.** **WHY THIS IS NOT A WORDING NIT:** the banner is a standing OPERATOR INSTRUCTION about whether an action touches the general ledger. An operator reading it will categorize freely believing it is metadata with no ledger effect — while in this entity **every categorize posts a balanced journal entry.** That is the opposite of what the screen promises, on the one axis where being wrong matters most. **It is also the EXACT error the accounting standard warns about** (quoted there against revenue-recognition: *"Reading the global `default_enabled=false` and concluding OFF is a masked-scope error — read `lib.feature_flag_overrides` PER ENTITY"*) — **now baked into the banking UI, in the place an operator is most likely to trust.** **METHOD NOTE — THE INTENT TEST DID NOT EXCUSE THIS.** Earlier this session I correctly declined to file the vendor-credit GL gap because the page declared it AND prod corroborated the declaration (flag genuinely off). **A declaration only earns deference when it is TRUE.** Here the same check found prod contradicting it, so the declaration IS the defect. **The test is "verify the declaration", not "trust the declaration."** **FIX:** render the per-entity override, not the global default — ideally the LIVE state ("bank-feed GL posting is ON for this entity — categorizing posts a balanced JE") so it cannot drift again; static copy asserting a flag state will always rot. **GUARD:** assert no UI string claims a money-posting flag is OFF while any per-entity override for it is true. **Recorded by the live-verifier lane, which does not build.** Full record: `docs/audit/LIVE-TXN-BATTERY-2026-08-06.md` item 62. | — | **OPEN (major) — live-proven 2026-08-07** |

| **★ CORRECTED BY ITS AUTHOR — retitled** `LV-TRK-AP-SPLIT-ACROSS-TWO-ACTIVE-ACCOUNTS` (was `LV-AP-POSTED-TO-DEACTIVATED-ROLE-ACCOUNT`) — **TRK A/P is SPLIT: $300.01 sits on a DEACTIVATED `ap_control` account, $25.00 on the active one; any correct role-based A/P report silently omits the $300.01** | — | C | **CC-1 / money** | **LIVE-PROVEN 2026-08-07 (CC-3 live-verifier lane), Neon prod, read-only. STRUCTURAL only — no numeric QBO-vs-TMS comparison, per the standing rule that FY2023–2025 figures are still moving.** `accounting.chart_of_accounts_roles` moved TRKs `ap_control` to **`TRK-2000`** at **2026-06-16 04:42:46**, deactivating **`2000`** at the same instant (superseded by DEACTIVATION, never deletion — WORM correctly applied). **Yet A/P postings landed on BOTH afterwards:** `2000` (deactivated) on **2026-07-12** ($0.01, *"GUARD TRK posting test — void me"*) and **2026-07-29** ($300.00, *"Prepaid purchase GUARD prepaid first-JE"*) — **26 and 43 days AFTER the deactivation, so this is NOT historical residue** — while the one clearly-production posting (a bill, 2026-08-03, $25.00) correctly used the active `TRK-2000`. **So the BILL poster honours the registry.** **★ WHAT I WILL NOT CLAIM:** both `2000` postings carry **"GUARD"** in their memo, marking them as verification-lane artifacts rather than business transactions. I therefore CANNOT distinguish **(a)** a posting path resolving `2000` from the registry and ignoring `is_active=false` — **a real defect** — from **(b)** a guard test naming the account explicitly, which is **expected state**. **UNVERIFIED, and not guessed.** Resolving it is a bounded code read of the prepaid-purchase posting path (does it resolve `ap_control`, or take an account id from its caller?) and belongs to the lane that owns the poster. **★ WHAT IS ESTABLISHED EITHER WAY:** **TRKs A/P is genuinely split — $300.01 on a deactivated control account, $25.00 on the active one.** Any report, aging or tie-out that resolves A/P through the ACTIVE `ap_control` role — the correct way to resolve it — **silently omits $300.01**, regardless of how the rows got there, and it will not correct itself. **FIX:** decide and act — if they are test artifacts, VOID them per WORM (they are on the ledger, so they count); if a path resolves the role without honouring `is_active`, fix that path. **Either way TRK A/P must end up on ONE account.** **GUARD:** assert **no posting targets a `chart_of_accounts_roles` account whose binding is `is_active = false`** — a clean, permanent, entity-agnostic invariant that catches this class before it splits a control account again. **ALSO RESOLVED HERE (third UNVERIFIED retired):** item 64 flagged that USMCA posts A/R to `1100` while the locked mapping says `QBO-45`. **Correct by configuration** — USMCAs ACTIVE `ar_control` IS `1100` and `ap_control` IS `2000`, so the poster faithfully honours the registry; the locked "A/R = QBO-45 / A/P = QBO-47" statement is **TRANSP-specific** (and TRANSP does exactly that — 1,550 A/P postings on `QBO-47`). USMCA has no QBO realm, so native numbering is right. **NOT a defect.** **★★ SELF-CORRECTION 2026-08-07 (item 67) — MY FRAMING AND MY PROPOSED GUARD WERE BOTH WRONG. READ THIS BEFORE ACTING ON THE ROW ABOVE.** I generalised the measurement across all entities/roles and got **154 postings** to accounts with an inactive role binding — which looked like a large live class. **It is not.** **`chart_of_accounts_roles.is_active=false` means "no longer THE designated account for THAT ROLE" — it does NOT mean the account is deactivated or unpostable.** `QBO-1150040141` is a **BANK account** (and still holds 1 ACTIVE role binding) — 142 postings is normal banking; `5400 Truck Repairs & Maintenance` is an **EXPENSE account** — 8 repair postings is normal expense activity. **Verified on `catalogs.accounts.deactivated_at`: every one of those accounts is ACTIVE.** **★ THE DECISIVE QUERY IS A PASS:** postings landing on an account AFTER its own `deactivated_at` = **ZERO**, database-wide. The system is correct on the invariant that actually matters (TRANSPs `2000` was deactivated 2026-06-30 and has received nothing since). **=> MY PROPOSED GUARD IS WITHDRAWN.** Built as specified it would have reddened on **154 CORRECT postings** — a permanently-red guard, which then gets muted; the exact failure mode flagged for the tie-out guard in item 64. **Twice now a guard-spec error was caught by measuring BEFORE it was built.** **THE CORRECT GUARD** is the one the data already passes: **assert no posting lands on an account after its own `deactivated_at`** — meaningful, currently green, catches a real regression. **★ WHAT SURVIVES, RESTATED HONESTLY:** TRKs A/P sits on **TWO ACTIVE A/P ACCOUNTS** — `2000` "Accounts Payable" ($300.01) and `TRK-2000` "Accounts Payable" ($25.00) — with `ap_control` designating `TRK-2000`. **A role-based A/P report resolves `TRK-2000` and omits $300.01.** That split is real and should be resolved; the cause is **two active A/P accounts in one entity**, NOT posting to a disabled account. **LESSON (reusable): a flag on a JOIN table (`role.is_active`) describes THE RELATIONSHIP, never the ROW it points at.** **Recorded by the live-verifier lane, which does not build.** Full record: `docs/audit/LIVE-TXN-BATTERY-2026-08-06.md` items 66 + 67. | — | **OPEN (minor) — corrected + narrowed 2026-08-07** |

| **★ OPEN (P0 · BLOCKER)** `LV-WO-CREATE-500-OPENED-AT` — **work-order creation is 100% broken for EVERY entity; two writers in one request disagree and trip the table's own immutability trigger** | — | C | **mechanical / backend lane — ROUTING CONFLICT NAMED: standing-order rule 7 says mechanical → CC-2, owner-locked board §3 says CC-2 NEVER BUILDS and assigns mechanical to CC-3, which also never builds. Whichever lane actually builds must take this — it must not be stranded.** | **REPRODUCED LIVE TWICE through the product's own endpoint (USMCA, 2026-08-07), captured with a fetch interceptor in the authed session:** `POST /api/v1/maintenance/work-orders` → **HTTP 500** `{"code":"P0001","message":"E_WO_OPENED_AT_IMMUTABLE: opened_at cannot be changed once set"}`. **MY FIRST HYPOTHESIS WAS WRONG AND IS WITHDRAWN** — I said the trigger "fires on INSERT where there is no prior value"; reading the function on prod disproved it. `maintenance.wo_set_opened_at` is **CORRECT**: it sets `opened_at` on INSERT when NULL and raises ONLY on `TG_OP=UPDATE AND OLD.opened_at IS DISTINCT FROM NEW.opened_at`. **The trigger is the sensor, not the bug.** **ACTUAL ROOT CAUSE** (`apps/backend/src/maintenance/two-section-service.ts`): **:203 INSERT** writes `opened_at = COALESCE($8::timestamptz, now())` from `header.service_date`, then **:285 post-insert UPDATE** writes `opened_at = COALESCE($1, opened_at)` from `header.opened_at`. Both are present in a normal form submit and they always differ (a date at midnight vs a timestamp) → the UPDATE changes a column the INSERT already set → RAISE → whole transaction aborts → 500, no row. The comment at **:272-273** shows the author saw the hazard ("All COALESCE so a missing field never clobbers the INSERT defaults (e.g. opened_at)") — but COALESCE only protects when the field is ABSENT, never when it is PRESENT AND DIFFERENT, which is the normal case from this form. **LIVE DB, prod `br-fancy-credit-akjnd07a`, completeness discriminator SATISFIED** (`current_user=neondb_owner` asserted in-statement; `count(*)`=1 == `n_live_tup`=1; positive control on the SAME table): `n_tup_ins`=**13**, `n_tup_del`=**3**, `n_live_tup`=**1**, USMCA visible=**0**, trigger `trg_wo_set_opened_at` on INS/UPD. **13 − 3 = 10 expected live but 1 is live → NINE inserts rolled back** = the durable signature of aborted transactions, the IDENTICAL shape as `LV-TXN-014` (insurance create). **THIS IS A CLASS, NOT AN INCIDENT** — post-insert UPDATE colliding with a column guard. **FIX THE ROOT CAUSE, NOT THE SENSOR: do NOT relax, drop or except `wo_set_opened_at`** — it guards an audit-relevant timestamp and is behaving correctly. Settle ONE source of truth: drop `opened_at` from the :285 SET list and have the INSERT take `header.opened_at ?? header.service_date`. **Blocks the entire maintenance chain** (WO → parts+labor → close → bill) and the maintenance half of the USMCA battery. Full detail: `docs/audit/LIVE-TXN-BATTERY-2026-08-06.md` item 73. | **OPEN** |
| **★ OPEN (P0 · money)** `LV-WO-RECONCILE-EXCLUDES-SECTION-A` — **the work order certifies "Reconciled — WO parts & labor tie to the vendor invoice" while its own total is $436.66 ABOVE the amount actually reconciled** | — | C | **CC-1 (money / GL / A-P)** | **VERIFIED LIVE IN THE PRODUCT, USMCA, 2026-08-07.** One work order, final state: Subtotal A `$289.45` + Subtotal B `$289.45` = `$578.90`, tax 8.25% `$47.76`, **WO Total = A + B = `$626.66`**, registering as a **Bill (A/P)** ("payable later, 1099-tracked"). Vendor Invoice Reconcile at that same moment: Parts WO total `$0.00`, Labor WO total `$190.00`, invoice entered `$190.00` → panel prints **"Reconciled — WO parts & labor tie to the vendor invoice."** and **ALL NINE pre-save checks go ✓**, including "Vendor invoice reconciles". **ROOT CAUSE — the two totals are computed from DISJOINT SETS** (read in code, not inferred; `apps/backend/src/maintenance/two-section-service.ts:186-191`): `sectionATotal` = Σ category lines; `sectionBTotal` = Σ `Math.max(line.amount, Σ sub_rows.amount)`; `totalCost = A + B`. The reconcile reads **only the parts/labor sub-rows**, so Section A category lines — and any parent item-line amount exceeding its children — are **structurally invisible** to the reconcile while being fully included in the WO total and the A/P bill. A user can put the whole repair in Section A, enter a `$0.00` vendor invoice, and the screen still certifies "Reconciled." **WHY THIS IS WORSE THAN NO CONTROL:** the reconcile is the control that proves a maintenance bill matches the vendor's actual invoice — precisely what a CPA, auditor, insurer or bankruptcy examiner tests. Certifying a tie while $436.66 of the same document sits outside its scope manufactures documentary assurance that is false. All correct; none matched a single predicate. **CONSEQUENCE: a guard for "money totals exclude voided rows" cannot be written with acceptable precision today** — any pattern either misses real defects or reddens on correct code, and a guard that is wrong more often than right trains people to ignore it. **The precise, table-aware line-table guard already shipped (`verify-money-line-sums-exclude-voided.mjs`, ACCT-F156) stands and is NOT affected** — it is scoped to 3 tables with verified per-table columns and has zero false positives. **The real fix is canonicalisation, not a guard:** one predicate per table recorded in a machine-readable map (or a `*_active` view per financial table), after which the broad guard becomes trivial and precise. | **RULING** `CLS-MONEY-HOLD` / `HOLD-001` — the card is **STALE**: settlements are no longer 0, the pipeline FIRES, and the $0.00 settlement is arithmetically CORRECT | — | C | **CC-1 (money) — closed by ruling** | **VERIFIED on prod 2026-08-07** (RLS-bypassed, `visible == n_live_tup` on each table). The card says *"driver_settlements = 0, flags ON but no posting output"*. **All three clauses are now false.** `driver_finance.driver_settlements` = **1** (not 0); `accounting.load_revenue_recognition_postings` = **2** against **2** delivered loads — a clean **1:1**, so revenue recognition is demonstrably firing. **THE ONE SETTLEMENT IS CORRECT, PROVEN BY ARITHMETIC not assertion:** `S-LUSMCAFREIGHT-20260806-0001`, `status=closed`, `approval_status=needs_review`, gross $0.00 / deductions $0.00 / net $0.00, 0 lines, `trip_closed_at` set. I nearly filed this as a defect at the point where I confirmed **91 ACTIVE driver pay rates exist** and the settlement's own driver has one — config is NOT missing. The miles check is what resolved it: the rate is **`basis_type=per_mile_pay`, 48¢/mile, `miles_basis=short_miles`**, and the bookended load `LUSMCAFREIGHT-20260806-0001` carries **`loaded_miles`, `miles_practical` AND `miles_deadhead` all NULL**. **48¢ x zero miles = $0.00.** The settlement did not fail to compute; it computed correctly on a load with no mileage. `needs_review` is the RIGHT outcome — the system flagged a $0.00 settlement for a human instead of silently paying it. The pay rate also carries **`is_test_data = true`**, consistent with PERMANENT LAW §4 (all TMS-native data is test). **VERDICT: EXPECTED STATE. Do not 'fix' the $0.00** — forcing a non-zero settlement would invent driver pay from miles that do not exist, which is the same prohibition as backfilling a load FK or auto-categorising bank rows. **REAL SUB-FINDING worth its own card, and it is a UX/data-quality gap, NOT a money defect (the money is right):** a per-mile rate against a load with NULL miles yields $0.00 with nothing telling the reviewer WHY. `needs_review` says *look at this*; it does not say *the rate is per-mile and the load has no miles*. The settlement surface should state the reason. **Routed to CC-2 (settlement UI is mechanical lane); the money side needs no change.** | ruling | — |
| **OPEN (P0 — blocks Layer 2)** `CI-F26` — **splitting `build-typecheck` CANNOT be done by classifying steps: 1,412 verify-steps exist and exactly ONE declares a DB capability** | — | C | **CC-1 (CI lane)** | **Layer 3 of the no-collision system is the hard prerequisite for Layer 2** (strict up-to-date), because at an 18-min recheck against a ~17.8-min mean merge interval — measured 2026-08-07: `build-typecheck` succeeds in 18 min, 81 merges to main in 24h — strict up-to-date LIVELOCKS rather than serializes. **MEASURED BLOCKER:** `scripts/verify-steps/` holds **1,412** step files and `grep` for `requiresDb|needsDb|capability` matches **1**. There is no metadata to split on, so a fast/slow split by step is not achievable without classifying 1,412 steps by hand — and a wrong classification either reddens CI or, far worse, produces a fast REQUIRED gate that silently skips real checks while looking green, which is the fake-green failure the whole guard system exists to prevent. **THE VIABLE SPLIT, and it needs no classification:** the repo already has `npm run verify:static`, which runs every static guard against a dead-port sentinel with no DB and reports only guards CI actually runs — it is what CC-1 has used as the pre-push gate all session. So the split is **static vs DB-backed**, not fast-steps vs slow-steps: `build-typecheck-fast` = `npm ci` + tsc + `verify:static` (REQUIRED, queue-friendly); `migration-replay` = the Postgres service + `verify:pre-commit` + backend db.tests (its own check, REQUIRED on money/migration PRs, informational elsewhere). **THE TRADE MUST BE STATED, NOT HIDDEN:** the fast gate does NOT cover the DB-backed guards, so making only it required REDUCES coverage on the required path. That is acceptable ONLY if `migration-replay` stays REQUIRED on any PR touching `db/migrations/**` or the financial cluster — otherwise a money PR could merge on a gate that never touched a database. **DO NOT enable strict up-to-date until this lands and the fast gate's CI duration is MEASURED, not assumed fast.** | — | — |
